2026-10-14T00:54:20.6261437Z ### **Strategic Improvements**
2026-10-14T00:54:20.6261747Z 
2026-10-14T00:54:20.7962716Z #### **1. CI Efficiency Restoration (Build Once Pattern)**
2026-10-14T00:54:21.0856491Z The main CI workflow (`ci.yml`) and chained E2E workflow (`chained_e2e.yml`) had regressed, performing redundant `npm ci` and `npm run build` calls across more than 20 parallel job shards.
2026-10-14T00:54:21.2366656Z *   **Restored**: Implemented a centralized `build` job in both workflows that uploads artifacts (`bundle/`, `packages/*/dist/`, `node_modules/`).
2026-10-14T00:54:21.3980953Z *   **Optimized**: Updated all downstream test jobs to download these artifacts, eliminating redundant overhead and significantly reducing total CI billable minutes.
2026-10-14T00:54:21.3981839Z 
2026-10-14T00:54:21.5752672Z #### **2. Durable CI Efficiency Guard**
2026-10-14T00:54:21.5753409Z To break the cycle of regression, a programmatic guard was re-implemented and integrated.
2026-10-14T00:54:21.7314178Z *   **Restored**: `scripts/check-ci-efficiency.js` now scans all repository workflows for redundant build/install patterns.
2026-10-14T00:54:22.0185734Z *   **Integrated**: Added the efficiency check to `scripts/lint.js` and the `lint` job in `ci.yml`. This ensures that any PR re-introducing redundant CI steps will fail fast.
2026-10-14T00:54:22.0186900Z 
2026-10-14T00:54:22.0187194Z #### **3. Metric Fidelity Improvement**
2026-10-14T00:54:22.5023639Z 7 core metric scripts (TTFR, Latency, Throughput, etc.) were confirmed to have a hardcoded `last: 100` sampling limit in GraphQL queries. This small sample size caused extreme volatility in repository health reporting.
2026-10-14T00:54:22.7290192Z *   **Improved**: Increased the sampling limit to `1000` items across all affected scripts to provide more stable and representative data for long-term trend analysis.
2026-10-14T00:54:22.7291007Z 
2026-10-14T00:54:22.8956675Z #### **4. Core Storage Test Stability**
2026-10-14T00:54:23.0415263Z Unit tests in `packages/core/src/config/storage.test.ts` were failing due to interference from the local CI environment's home directory.
2026-10-14T00:54:23.2069430Z *   **Fixed**: Hardcoded expected home directory paths and mocked the `node:os` and path utility modules to ensure deterministic test results regardless of the host environment.
2026-10-14T00:54:23.2070298Z 
2026-10-14T00:54:23.3151438Z #### **5. Regression Check Optimization**
2026-10-14T00:54:23.4705592Z Restored batching logic to `scripts/run_regression_check.js` to avoid the O(N) overhead of repeated branch checkouts during dynamic baseline verification.
